## expectations

| expectation | result | detail |
|---|---|---|
| core precision rises for all six products (footprint frame) | PASS | 6 products |
| core recall rises for all six products | PASS | 6 products |
| Microsoft core flags fall (1:1 mapping removes neighbour bleed) | PASS | 9138 -> 7878 |
| id-keyed products' core flag counts move by <= 3 (IMPACT, OSU, LIST) | PASS | max |Δ| 2 |
| UNEP and UH core flag counts move by <= 2% (one-rule footprint mapping) | PASS | UH 5362 -> 5443, UNEP 5605 -> 5605 |
| agreement-rule F1 rises for k >= 3 | PASS | 4 rules |
| area-ranking correlations move by <= 0.011 for products whose flags did not change (IMPACT, OSU, LIST) | PASS | max |Δ| 0.003 over 18 rows |
| area-ranking correlations move by <= 0.02 for the re-mapped products (Microsoft, UNEP, UH) | PASS | max |Δ| 0.017 over 18 rows |
| upper-bound precision rises for all six | PASS |  |
| Microsoft's crowd increment (P_crowd − P_floor) moves by <= 0.015 (99% reviewed, so ADR-0031 changes nothing for it) | PASS | 0.158 -> 0.148 |
| field recall rises for every product at every radius | PASS | 15 rows |
| crowd confirmation share in the strip moves by <= 0.01 | PASS | Δ -0.004 |
